# Win-probability model (batch 10, 181 matches)

Every number is win-chance points for an otherwise even game (50%), learned from the matches so far; "+-" is the uncertainty across bags. Few matches = rough curves.

## How well it predicts (matches held out whole)

**Verdict: beats strength alone (clear: the range excludes 0).** Team strength alone is the baseline; the curves below are what the game state adds to it.

Gain over strength alone (log loss, resampling whole matches): +0.119, 90% range +0.017 to +0.214.

- strength only: accuracy 77%, log loss 0.433 (0-5 min 83%; 5-10 min 79%; 10-15 min 72%; 15-end min 65%; matches lasting 15 min+ 70%; matches even on strength (gap under 200) 57%)
- glass box: accuracy 87%, log loss 0.314 (0-5 min 86%; 5-10 min 87%; 10-15 min 84%; 15-end min 90%; matches lasting 15 min+ 84%; matches even on strength (gap under 200) 75%)
- glass box without corrections: accuracy 86%, log loss 0.344 (0-5 min 85%; 5-10 min 87%; 10-15 min 84%; 15-end min 88%; matches lasting 15 min+ 82%; matches even on strength (gap under 200) 74%)
- black box: accuracy 82%, log loss 0.637 (0-5 min 84%; 5-10 min 82%; 10-15 min 79%; 15-end min 79%; matches lasting 15 min+ 78%; matches even on strength (gap under 200) 73%)

## Corrections applied

- lord_damage_diff & minute: interaction (Denning (agreeing with Claude's batch 3 finding): Lord damage only decides a match at 28:00 (the tiebreak); before that a team can deal a lot of Lord damage and still lose to a Lord kill, so Lord damage should be read together with the clock.)
- lord_dps_own: cap (Denning (2026-11-07, agreeing with Claude's batch 7 finding): each team's Lord damage per second is fixed by its build, so it is one value per team per match; the curves zigzagged in mirror image, each step resting on 11 to 14 team-matches with no trend in win rate (memorizing which teams won). Held-out log loss on 91 matches: as before 0.404, both dropped 0.413, directions 0.393, cap at 5 points 0.377 (best), cap + directions 0.384.)
- lord_dps_enemy: cap (Denning (2026-11-07, agreeing with Claude's batch 7 finding): each team's Lord damage per second is fixed by its build, so it is one value per team per match; the curves zigzagged in mirror image, each step resting on 11 to 14 team-matches with no trend in win rate (memorizing which teams won). Held-out log loss on 91 matches: as before 0.404, both dropped 0.413, directions 0.393, cap at 5 points 0.377 (best), cap + directions 0.384.)
- healing_monks_own: drop (Denning (2026-11-07, agreeing with Claude's batch 8 finding): healing monks are one value per team per match and almost every team runs 3 (0 monks: 1 team-match, 4 monks: 5), so the curves' extreme steps rest on almost nothing and zigzag in mirror image. Held-out log loss on 113 matches: as before 0.385, cap at 5 points 0.382, dropped 0.367 (best).)
- healing_monks_enemy: drop (Denning (2026-11-07, agreeing with Claude's batch 8 finding): healing monks are one value per team per match and almost every team runs 3 (0 monks: 1 team-match, 4 monks: 5), so the curves' extreme steps rest on almost nothing and zigzag in mirror image. Held-out log loss on 113 matches: as before 0.385, cap at 5 points 0.382, dropped 0.367 (best).)
- lord_damage_diff: main_drop (Denning (agreeing with Claude's batch 9 review, 133 matches): the Lord damage curve on its own acts as a sign marker, not the 28:00 tiebreak: +-7 to 9 points once any Lord damage exists, the most negative band reads +31.6 (backwards), and teams ahead on Lord damage won 88% with alive +0.94 and morale +78 (it marks who got into the pit). Only its pairing with the clock is kept.)
- morale_diff: no_interaction (Denning (agreeing with Claude's batch 9 review): Lord damage per second and the spike build are fixed by the build, one value per team per match; paired with morale they memorize which teams won (morale x Lord damage per second came back 3rd and 4th, 0.42 each, despite the batch 7 caps on the main terms).)
- morale_total: no_interaction (Denning (agreeing with Claude's batch 9 review): as for morale_diff, build-fixed values are not paired with morale.)
- down_diff_s: direction (Denning (agreeing with Claude's batch 9 review): more enemy time down can only help; the curve read backwards for 7 to 38 s, mirroring alive_diff. Direction forced rather than dropping the variable.)
- flag_lead: direction (Denning (agreeing with Claude, second rebuild, 133 matches): a bigger flag lead can only help; the curve was small (at most 2.6 points) and ran backwards at both ends (-129 to -67.5 s read +0.4, +67.5 to +129 s read -1.6), so TQ 1 read 22 s of flag lead saved as +0.0 points.)
